- **pipeline-reviewer**: Fidelity judgment gate for promoted Metaflow flows in ml-lab investigations. Dispatched by the pipeline promotion workflow after a flow has been scaffolded and before it is run. Reads the promoted flow source alongside the investigation's source-of-truth document(s) and judges five intent-fidelity invariants: split convention, per-epoch reshuffle symmetry, axes-match-source, same-name/different-quantity metrics, and sweep-override inflation. Emits structured PASS/CONCERN/FAIL findings per rule. Does NOT run the flow; does NOT police DAG shape; does NOT duplicate mechanical linter checks. ([chris-santiago/ml-lab](https://github.com/chris-santiago/ml-lab/blob/HEAD/plugins/ml-lab/agents/pipeline-reviewer.md))
